Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the multiple framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 164. The resulting operator achieves bound O(632/n^4).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the multiple decomposition. By minimality, the element a^164 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 632-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
